<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01QFw59qVUueT7ErJjdd7ZWB`</summary>

````text
1	# ProofWriter 数据集调研报告
2	
3	## 1. 数据集基本信息
4	
5	**论文**: Tafjord, Dalvi, Clark. "ProofWriter: Generating Implications, Proofs, and Abductive Statements over Natural Language." Findings of ACL-IJCNLP 2021.
6	
7	**任务格式**: NLI 风格，与 FOLIO 一致
8	- **输入**: theory（自然语言事实 + 规则）+ question（待验证陈述）
9	- **输出**: True / False / Unknown（OWA 设定下）
10	- **附加**: 每个样本包含推理证明（DAG 格式）
11	
12	**数据规模**（OWA，与我们相关的设定）:
13	
14	| Depth | Theories | Questions |
15	|-------|----------|-----------|
16	| D0 | 26,978 | 100,000 |
17	| D1 | 12,933 | 100,014 |
18	| D2 | 9,033 | 100,010 |
19	| D3 | 6,940 | 100,036 |
20	| D5 | 4,752 | 100,030 |
21	
22	总计 ~500k questions (OWA)。Split: 70% train / 10% dev / 20% test。无 D4。
23	
24	**标签分布**:
25	- **OWA**: True / False / Unknown 各约 **33%**（均衡分布）
26	- **CWA**: 仅 True / False（无 Unknown），与我们不兼容
27	- 对比 FOLIO：~39% Unknown，ProofWriter 更均衡
28	
29	**4 种配置**: AttNeg（属性+否定）、AttNoneg（属性无否定）、RelNeg（关系+否定）、RelNoneg（关系无否定）
30	
31	## 2. 与 FOLIO 的对比
32	
33	| 维度 | ProofWriter | FOLIO |
34	|------|------------|-------|
35	| 生成方式 | **合成/模板化**（Datalog 程序生成） | **专家人工标注** |
36	| 词汇量 | ~101 词 | 4,351 词 |
37	| 自然语言风格 | 简单模板句："Charlie is quiet." "If someone is kind then they are young." | 真实自然语言，语义复杂 |
38	| 规模 | ~500k (OWA) | 1,435 样本 |
39	| 标签 | True/False/Unknown (OWA) | True/False/Unknown |
40	| FOL 标注 | 无 | 有 |
41	| 推理深度 | 显式标注 D0-D5 | 隐式（范围 0-14） |
42	| 证明 | 包含（DAG） | 无 |
43	
44	**核心差异**: ProofWriter 是合成数据，语言高度模板化，推理结构干净可控；FOLIO 是真实自然语言，语义歧义多，形式化难度高。两者互补性强。
45	
46	**FOL 形式化适配性**: ProofWriter 的规则结构极其规整（一阶 Horn 子句 + 否定），转 Z3 非常直接。事实是简单的一元/二元谓词，规则是标准的 if-then 形式。比 FOLIO 容易得多。
47	
48	**Python 代码形式化适配性**: 同样容易。事实可表示为集合/字典，规则可表示为函数或推理循环。模板化结构意味着 LLM 生成代码的变异性更低。
49	
50	## 3. 对我们方法的预期影响
51	
52	### 3.1 不对称性预测
53	
54	**核心问题**: ProofWriter 会放大还是缩小 FOLIO 上观察到的 9:1 FOL 偏向不对称性？
55	
56	**预测: disagree rate 会显著降低，不对称性模式可能改变。**
57	
58	理由：
59	- ProofWriter 的模板化语言大幅降低了 NL→FOL 和 NL→Code 的翻译难度
60	- FOL 和 Code 都会变得更准确 → agree rate 上升 → disagree 样本减少
61	- FOLIO 的 9:1 不对称性很可能源于 FOLIO 的语义复杂性对 Code 形式化的挑战更大（自然语言歧义 → Code 更容易出错），而 ProofWriter 消除了这一因素
62	
63	**两种可能的结果**:
64	1. **不对称性缩小**（如 6:4 或 5:5）：说明不对称性与数据集语言复杂度相关，非方法固有偏差 → 对论文有利
65	2. **不对称性保持但方向可能翻转**：如果 Code 在结构化数据上反而更强 → 更有趣的发现
66	
67	### 3.2 推理深度作为分析轴
68	
69	**高价值**。可以回答：
70	- 浅层推理（D0-D1）是否 agree rate 极高（两种表征都轻松搞定）？
71	- 深层推理（D3-D5）是否 disagree rate 上升？哪种表征在深层推理更脆弱？
72	- 这为论文提供了 "推理复杂度 vs 表征分歧" 的分析维度，FOLIO 无法提供
73	
74	### 3.3 风险
75	
76	- **disagree 样本太少**: 如果两种表征在简单模板数据上都接近完美，disagree 数量可能不足以支撑统计分析
77	- **缓解**: 使用 D5（最深推理）+ RelNeg（关系+否定，最复杂配置）来最大化难度
78	
79	## 4. 实操信息
80	
81	### 4.1 HuggingFace 数据集
82	
83	**推荐: `smoorsmith/proofwriter`**
84	- 4,200 行（train 3,000 / dev 600 / test 600）
85	- OWA D5 子集，来自 Logic-LLM 仓库（与 FOLIO 评测对齐）
86	- 字段: `id`, `context`, `question`, `options`, `answer`, `depth`, `chain_of_thought`
87	- Logic-LLM 论文正是用 FOLIO + ProofWriter OWA-D5 做评测，有直接可比性
88	
89	**其他版本**:
90	- `tasksource/proofwriter`: 845k 行，全量数据，覆盖所有深度和配置
91	- `D3xter1922/proofwriter-dataset`: 59k 行，原始格式
92	
93	### 4.2 Pipeline 适配
94	
95	当前 FOLIO 格式: `{premises, conclusion, label}`
96	
97	ProofWriter (`smoorsmith/proofwriter`) 适配：
98	```
99	context → premises（事实+规则文本）
100	question → 需提取结论（去掉 "Based on the above information, is the following statement true, false, or unknown?" 前缀）
101	answer → label（True/False/Unknown）
102	depth → 额外元数据（用于分层分析）
103	```
104	
105	改动量小，新增一个 `ProofWriterDataLoader` 即可。
106	
107	### 4.3 推荐评测配置
108	
109	| 配置 | 样本数 | 理由 |
110	|------|--------|------|
111	| **OWA D5 test set** | 600 | Logic-LLM 标准配置，三类标签均衡，推理深度最大 |
112	| 如需缩减 | 随机采样 200-300 | 保持标签均衡采样 |
113	
114	**推荐方案**: 直接用 `smoorsmith/proofwriter` 的 600 test 样本，与 FOLIO 的 204 样本规模接近。
115	
116	## 5. 替代数据集
117	
118	| 数据集 | 标签 | 格式匹配度 | 优势 | 劣势 | 推荐度 |
119	|--------|------|-----------|------|------|--------|
120	| **ProofWriter OWA** | T/F/U | ★★★★★ | 标签一致、Logic-LLM 先例、深度轴 | 合成数据、语言简单 | **首选** |
121	| LogicNLI | E/C/N→T/F/U | ★★★★ | 三类标签、合成、FOL 基础 | 知名度低、无深度标注 | 备选 |
122	| RuleTaker | T/F | ★★★ | ProofWriter 前身、大规模 | 无 Unknown 类、仅 CWA | 不推荐 |
123	| LogiQA 2.0 NLI | 2-way | ★★ | 真实考试题 | 非规则推理、标签不匹配 | 不推荐 |
124	| CLUTRR | 关系推理 | ★ | 亲属关系推理 | 任务格式完全不同 | 不适用 |
125	| PrOntoQA | T/F | ★★ | 本体层级推理 | 无 Unknown、二分类 | 不推荐 |
126	
127	## 6. 结论
128	
129	**ProofWriter OWA D5 是最佳第二数据集**。理由：
130	1. 任务格式与 FOLIO 完全一致（premises → conclusion → T/F/U）
131	2. Logic-LLM 已建立 FOLIO + ProofWriter 的评测先例，审稿人容易接受
132	3. 合成 vs 人工标注的对比恰好验证方法在不同数据特性下的泛化性
133	4. 推理深度维度提供 FOLIO 无法提供的分析轴
134	5. 标签均衡（各33%）vs FOLIO 的不均衡（39% Unknown）可验证标签分布对不对称性的影响
135	6. HuggingFace 上有现成数据集（`smoorsmith/proofwriter`），适配工作量极小（~1天）
136	
````

</details>

      "content": "# ProofWriter 数据集调研报告\n\n## 1. 数据集基本信息\n\n**论文**: Tafjord, Dalvi, Clark. \"ProofWriter: Generating Implications, Proofs, and Abductive Statements over Natural Language.\" Findings of ACL-IJCNLP 2021.\n\n**任务格式**: NLI 风格，与 FOLIO 一致\n- **输入**: theory（自然语言事实 + 规则）+ question（待验证陈述）\n- **输出**: True / False / Unknown（OWA 设定下）\n- **附加**: 每个样本包含推理证明（DAG 格式）\n\n**数据规模**（OWA，与我们相关的设定）:\n\n| Depth | Theories | Questions |\n|-------|----------|-----------|\n| D0 | 26,978 | 100,000 |\n| D1 | 12,933 | 100,014 |\n| D2 | 9,033 | 100,010 |\n| D3 | 6,940 | 100,036 |\n| D5 | 4,752 | 100,030 |\n\n总计 ~500k questions (OWA)。Split: 70% train / 10% dev / 20% test。无 D4。\n\n**标签分布**:\n- **OWA**: True / False / Unknown 各约 **33%**（均衡分布）\n- **CWA**: 仅 True / False（无 Unknown），与我们不兼容\n- 对比 FOLIO：~39% Unknown，ProofWriter 更均衡\n\n**4 种配置**: AttNeg（属性+否定）、AttNoneg（属性无否定）、RelNeg（关系+否定）、RelNoneg（关系无否定）\n\n## 2. 与 FOLIO 的对比\n\n| 维度 | ProofWriter | FOLIO |\n|------|------------|-------|\n| 生成方式 | **合成/模板化**（Datalog 程序生成） | **专家人工标注** |\n| 词汇量 | ~101 词 | 4,351 词 |\n| 自然语言风格 | 简单模板句：\"Charlie is quiet.\" \"If someone is kind then they are young.\" | 真实自然语言，语义复杂 |\n| 规模 | ~500k (OWA) | 1,435 样本 |\n| 标签 | True/False/Unknown (OWA) | True/False/Unknown |\n| FOL 标注 | 无 | 有 |\n| 推理深度 | 显式标注 D0-D5 | 隐式（范围 0-14） |\n| 证明 | 包含（DAG） | 无 |\n\n**核心差异**: ProofWriter 是合成数据，语言高度模板化，推理结构干净可控；FOLIO 是真实自然语言，语义歧义多，形式化难度高。两者互补性强。\n\n**FOL 形式化适配性**: ProofWriter 的规则结构极其规整（一阶 Horn 子句 + 否定），转 Z3 非常直接。事实是简单的一元/二元谓词，规则是标准的 if-then 形式。比 FOLIO 容易得多。\n\n**Python 代码形式化适配性**: 同样容易。事实可表示为集合/字典，规则可表示为函数或推理循环。模板化结构意味着 LLM 生成代码的变异性更低。\n\n## 3. 对我们方法的预期影响\n\n### 3.1 不对称性预测\n\n**核心问题**: ProofWriter 会放大还是缩小 FOLIO 上观察到的 9:1 FOL 偏向不对称性？\n\n**预测: disagree rate 会显著降低，不对称性模式可能改变。**\n\n理由：\n- ProofWriter 的模板化语言大幅降低了 NL→FOL 和 NL→Code 的翻译难度\n- FOL 和 Code 都会变得更准确 → agree rate 上升 → disagree 样本减少\n- FOLIO 的 9:1 不对称性很可能源于 FOLIO 的语义复杂性对 Code 形式化的挑战更大（自然语言歧义 → Code 更容易出错），而 ProofWriter 消除了这一因素\n\n**两种可能的结果**:\n1. **不对称性缩小**（如 6:4 或 5:5）：说明不对称性与数据集语言复杂度相关，非方法固有偏差 → 对论文有利\n2. **不对称性保持但方向可能翻转**：如果 Code 在结构化数据上反而更强 → 更有趣的发现\n\n### 3.2 推理深度作为分析轴\n\n**高价值**。可以回答：\n- 浅层推理（D0-D1）是否 agree rate 极高（两种表征都轻松搞定）？\n- 深层推理（D3-D5）是否 disagree rate 上升？哪种表征在深层推理更脆弱？\n- 这为论文提供了 \"推理复杂度 vs 表征分歧\" 的分析维度，FOLIO 无法提供\n\n### 3.3 风险\n\n- **disagree 样本太少**: 如果两种表征在简单模板数据上都接近完美，disagree 数量可能不足以支撑统计分析\n- **缓解**: 使用 D5（最深推理）+ RelNeg（关系+否定，最复杂配置）来最大化难度\n\n## 4. 实操信息\n\n### 4.1 HuggingFace 数据集\n\n**推荐: `smoorsmith/proofwriter`**\n- 4,200 行（train 3,000 / dev 600 / test 600）\n- OWA D5 子集，来自 Logic-LLM 仓库（与 FOLIO 评测对齐）\n- 字段: `id`, `context`, `question`, `options`, `answer`, `depth`, `chain_of_thought`\n- Logic-LLM 论文正是用 FOLIO + ProofWriter OWA-D5 做评测，有直接可比性\n\n**其他版本**:\n- `tasksource/proofwriter`: 845k 行，全量数据，覆盖所有深度和配置\n- `D3xter1922/proofwriter-dataset`: 59k 行，原始格式\n\n### 4.2 Pipeline 适配\n\n当前 FOLIO 格式: `{premises, conclusion, label}`\n\nProofWriter (`smoorsmith/proofwriter`) 适配：\n```\ncontext → premises（事实+规则文本）\nquestion → 需提取结论（去掉 \"Based on the above information, is the following statement true, false, or unknown?\" 前缀）\nanswer → label（True/False/Unknown）\ndepth → 额外元数据（用于分层分析）\n```\n\n改动量小，新增一个 `ProofWriterDataLoader` 即可。\n\n### 4.3 推荐评测配置\n\n| 配置 | 样本数 | 理由 |\n|------|--------|------|\n| **OWA D5 test set** | 600 | Logic-LLM 标准配置，三类标签均衡，推理深度最大 |\n| 如需缩减 | 随机采样 200-300 | 保持标签均衡采样 |\n\n**推荐方案**: 直接用 `smoorsmith/proofwriter` 的 600 test 样本，与 FOLIO 的 204 样本规模接近。\n\n## 5. 替代数据集\n\n| 数据集 | 标签 | 格式匹配度 | 优势 | 劣势 | 推荐度 |\n|--------|------|-----------|------|------|--------|\n| **ProofWriter OWA** | T/F/U | ★★★★★ | 标签一致、Logic-LLM 先例、深度轴 | 合成数据、语言简单 | **首选** |\n| LogicNLI | E/C/N→T/F/U | ★★★★ | 三类标签、合成、FOL 基础 | 知名度低、无深度标注 | 备选 |\n| RuleTaker | T/F | ★★★ | ProofWriter 前身、大规模 | 无 Unknown 类、仅 CWA | 不推荐 |\n| LogiQA 2.0 NLI | 2-way | ★★ | 真实考试题 | 非规则推理、标签不匹配 | 不推荐 |\n| CLUTRR | 关系推理 | ★ | 亲属关系推理 | 任务格式完全不同 | 不适用 |\n| PrOntoQA | T/F | ★★ | 本体层级推理 | 无 Unknown、二分类 | 不推荐 |\n\n## 6. 结论\n\n**ProofWriter OWA D5 是最佳第二数据集**。理由：\n1. 任务格式与 FOLIO 完全一致（premises → conclusion → T/F/U）\n2. Logic-LLM 已建立 FOLIO + ProofWriter 的评测先例，审稿人容易接受\n3. 合成 vs 人工标注的对比恰好验证方法在不同数据特性下的泛化性\n4. 推理深度维度提供 FOLIO 无法提供的分析轴\n5. 标签均衡（各33%）vs FOLIO 的不均衡（39% Unknown）可验证标签分布对不对称性的影响\n6. HuggingFace 上有现成数据集（`smoorsmith/proofwriter`），适配工作量极小（~1天）\n",

```text
This is very important. Let me analyze the final gate results:

**mvp_pipeline_004 status: ANALYZING** (not DONE yet)

**Critical issue: Pipeline silently exited at sample 110!**
- Only 109/204 samples completed (53.4%, not 100%)
- Process silently exited during sample 110 (folio_validation_109 stability check)
- No error/traceback in log
- vLLM still running
- Instance not restarted (uptime 21d)

**MVP Gate Results (109 samples, All mode):**


All five criteria passed: C1 at 33.0%, C2 at 88.2%, C3 with 3 types, C4 showing an 11.6 percentage point improvement, and C5 at 81.8%.

When excluding unknown ground truth values, the results shift slightly—C1 drops to 32.8%, C2 improves to 90.9%, C5 reaches 92.9%, but C4 falls short with only a 3.6 percentage point gain, missing the 5 percentage point threshold. That's a critical failure since the pipeline only processed 53.4% of the data before silently exiting, so this "success" conclusion is built on incomplete results.

The pipeline exit without error logs is troubling—could be an out-of-memory issue, a Python segfault, or some unhandled exception. More importantly, C4's failure reveals that the targeted repair advantage is heavily concentrated on unknown-ground-truth samples; on known samples alone, it's only 3.6pp, but across all samples it jumps to 11.6pp because blind repair struggles much more with the hardest labels. C5 improved slightly with more data (now 22 samples instead of 15), though the confidence interval still hovers near the threshold.

The agent marked this as "success" based on the all-mode pass rate being 5/5, but we're only at 109/204 samples with partial data, and C4 fails when excluding unknown labels—so calling this a success feels premature. The report and roadmap are both stale now, and I need to check the server logs to understand why the pipeline exited silently.

_source line 330 · 2026-05-14T15:55:29.071Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have the full picture:

1. **PID 29177 is dead** — process terminated
2. **JSON has 109 entries** (109 folio_validation_ matches, 218 ground_truth matches = 109 samples × 2 contexts per sample? No, 218 is weird. Actually `ground_truth` appears twice per sample — once in the top-level record and once in the disagreement dict. So 218/2 = 109 samples. OK.)
3. **JSON ends cleanly** — `]` at the end, so the file is not corrupted


4. **Last save was at 23:42:20** (sample 108 done), then the process continued to sample 109 but crashed during the stability check around 23:45:42 without persisting that final record.

So I have 109 completed and saved samples. The process died partway through sample 110. Now looking at the MVP gate analysis across these 109 samples — all five criteria in the "all mode" category are passing, with C1 at 33.0% (34/103) exceeding the 10% threshold, C2 at 88.2% (30/34) exceeding 40%, and C3 covering three types.

When I exclude the unknown ground truth cases, I'm getting four out of five criteria passing, but C4 fails — it's only showing a +3.6 percentage point improvement, which falls short of the 5pp requirement. The other metrics look solid: C1 at 32.8%, C2 at 90.9%, C3 with two types, and C5 at 92.9%.

The main issue I'm flagging is that I'm only at 53.4% of the full dataset (109 out of 204 samples), so drawing firm conclusions from partial data is premature.

The silent exit without any error message is troubling — could be an out-of-memory issue during the stability check phase, a Python crash, a vLLM connection problem, or the system's OOM killer terminating the process. I should check system logs for OOM messages and consider resuming the pipeline to get the remaining 95 samples.

The C4 failure when excluding unknown ground truth is significant because it reveals the targeted repair advantage drops substantially on known cases — from +11.6pp overall down to just +3.6pp — suggesting the improvement is concentrated in the unknown ground truth subset rather than being broadly applicable. the benefit is disproportionately from Unknown-GT samples
   - On known-GT samples, both targeted and blind repair perform similarly (~64-68%)
   - This could be because: (a) Unknown samples are genuinely harder and targeted info helps more, or (b) the targeted repair is effectively guessing "Unknown" more often based on the error diagnosis
   - **This is a potential audit concern** — if the paper claims "targeted repair significantly outperforms blind", a reviewer could point out that this only holds when including Unknown-GT samples, not for True/False classification
   
4. **Agent labeled conclusion as "success"** — This is premature:
   - Data is partial (109/204)
   - C4 fails in excl-Unknown mode
   - "success" implies the MVP gate is fully passed, which is not the case with partial data

5. **C5 improved from 80% (n=15) at 83 samples to 81.8% (n=22) at 109 samples** — Stable and now marginally more confident. Wilson 95% CI for 18/22 ≈ [60%, 94%], lower bound right at the threshold.

6. **C4 is showing concerning erosion** — The targeted repair advantage dropped from +21.9pp at 83 samples to +11.6pp at 109 samples, with blind repair steadily catching up. If this downward trend continues through the full 204 samples, C4 could fall below the 5pp threshold.

I'm now looking at the performance breakdown between the new samples added from 83 to 109 — in that batch, blind repair actually outperformed targeted repair (72.7% vs 54.5%), which is a reversal of the overall trend and suggests the advantage is weakening. This is a warning-level finding because C4 could potentially fail at full scale, and it's already failing in the excl-Unknown mode at +3.6pp.

Now I need to reconcile this with the MVP gate criteria: all 5 criteria pass in All mode, but only 4/5 pass in excl-Unknown mode since C4 fails. According to D002, this grey-zone scenario where the primary mode passes but the secondary mode fails should be resolved by testing against a second dataset, though there's a possibility this could be acceptable pending ProofWriter confirmation.
```

Unknown mode (n=67): 4/5 PASS, C4 FAIL (+3.6pp)**\\n- Pipeline 可 --resume 续跑剩余 95 样本\\n\\n## v4 truncation fix 是 MVP 通过的关键（2026-05-14）\\n\\nv4 添加 finish_reason='length' 截断检测 + enable_thinking=False 重试，**同时解决了 FOL 和 Code 端的 crash/empty 问题**。80/204 中间数据 MVP 5/5 PASS（C1 32%, C2 87.5%, C3 3 types, C4 +22.6pp, C5 100%）。从 v1(2/5)→v2(3/5)→v3(1/5) 到 v4(5/5) 的突破。\\n\\n## Code Unknown 是 lazy prompt 问题，非模型能力边界（2026-05-14）\\n\\n46.3% Code=Unknown 的根因：LLM 生成代码主动 print(\\\"Unknown\\\") 而非执行错误。两种模式：(A) 硬编码放弃（注释推理后直接 print Unknown）；(B) 条件 fallback（else 分支用 Unknown 替代 False）。v5b prompt 已设计（anti-lazy Unknown + 禁止 hardcode + 3 个 few-shot）待 v4 完成后验证。\\n\\n## FOL 在 FOLIO 上压倒性优于 Code（2026-05-14）\\n\\nDisagree 样本中 FOL 正确 78.3% vs Code 仅 8.7%。Z3 形式化在结构化逻辑推理上远强于 Python enumeration。这意味着 disagreement 的诊断方向主要是\\\"Code 错 FOL 对\\\"，targeted repair 主要修 Code 端。\\n\\n## 禁止双进程启动 pipeline（2026-05-14，教训）\\n\\nsubmit_on_allocated（tmux）和 nohup 同时启动了两个 pipeline 实例，写同一输出文件，导致竞争条件 + 数据损坏 + pipeline 静默退出。**启动 pipeline 只用一种方式，确认前一个进程已终止后再启动新的。**\\n\\n## v5 closed-world 2-way 方案被撤回（D007, 2026-05-14）\\n\\nReviewer 指出强制 2-way 会在 ~39% Unknown-GT 样本上制造人为 disagreement（FOL=Unknown vs Code=False），污染诊断信号。**改为 v5b：保持 3-way + anti-lazy Unknown 约束**，确保 disagreement 信号来自推理差异而非假设差异。\\n\\n## Qwen3-32B-AWQ baseline 精度（2026-05-14）\\n\\nFOLIO validation set direct answer accuracy = 74%（50 samples），满足 ≤85% 门控。\\n\\n## Code 端 thinking token 泄漏已解决（2026-05-14）\\n\\nQwen3 系列模型默认开启 thinking mode，Code generation 时 thinking tokens 泄漏到代码输出导致解析崩溃。解决：`enable_thinking=False` + `_strip_thinking`。v3 验证 code crash 40%→0%。\\n\", \"total_chars\": 1901, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I can see the key changes:

1. **`_load_done_ids(path)` function (line 34)**: Loads only IDs from the resume file, not full records. This addresses the memory issue.

2. **JSONL format**: `json.dumps(rec) + '\n'` (lines 66, 243) — each sample written as one JSON line, not as a JSON array. This is the streaming write approach.

3. **`gc.collect()`**: Called at lines 68 (after each record in conversion?) and 247 (after each sample processing).

4. **`done_ids` set**: Only stores string IDs, not full result objects.
   
There's a format compatibility issue here — the output switched from a JSON array to JSONL, but `evaluate_mvp.py` still uses `json.load()` which expects valid JSON. I need to check whether the two write operations at lines 66 and 243 serve different purposes, and whether the evaluation script has been updated to handle the new format.
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"    log.info(\\n        \\\"[%s] done  FOL=%s  Code=%s  GT=%s  type=%s  cost=$%.4f\\\",\\n        example.id, fol_ex.verdict, code_ex.verdict,\\n        example.label, dis.agreement_type, rec[\\\"api_usage\\\"][\\\"cost_usd\\\"],\\n    )\\n    return rec\\n\\n\\ndef run_pipeline(\\n    max_examples: Optional[int] = None,\\n    output_file: Optional[str] = None,\\n    resume_from: Optional[str] = None,\\n):\\n\\n    examples = load_folio(max_examples=max_examples)\\n    log.info(\\\"loaded %d FOLIO examples\\\", len(examples))\\n\\n    done_ids: set = set()\\n\\n    if resume_from and Path(resume_from).exists():\\n        done_ids = _load_done_ids(resume_from)\\n        log.info(\\\"resumed %d already-done examples\\\", len(done_ids))\\n\\n        if output_file is None:\\n            stem = Path(resume_from).stem\\n            output_file = str(RESULTS_DIR / f\\\"{stem}.jsonl\\\")\\n\\n        # Convert JSON array → JSONL if needed\\n        if not Path(output_file).exists():\\n            with open(resume_from) as f:\\n                first_char = f.read(1)\\n            if first_char == '[':\\n                _convert_json_to_jsonl(resume_from, output_file)\\n                log.info(\\\"converted %s -> %s\\\", resume_from, output_file)\\n\\n    if output_file is None:\\n        ts = time.strftime(\\\"%Y%m%d_%H%M%S\\\")\\n        output_file = str(RESULTS_DIR / f\\\"results_{ts}.jsonl\\\")\\n\\n    for i, ex in enumerate(examples, 1):\\n        if ex.id in done_ids:\\n            continue\\n\\n        log.info(\\\"[%d/%d] processing %s …\\\", i, len(examples), ex.id)\\n        try:\\n            rec = process_one(ex)\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            log.error(\\\"[%s] pipeline error: %s\\\", ex.id, e)\\n            rec = {\\n                \\\"id\\\": ex.id,\\n                \\\"premises\\\": ex.premises,\\n                \\\"conclusion\\\": ex.conclusion,\\n                \\\"ground_truth\\\": ex.label,\\n                \\\"pipeline_error\\\": str(e),\\n            }\\n\\n        # The process has been running for 8 minutes 40 seconds now, but I saw it at 6 minutes 25 seconds earlier.

Now I'm checking whether the file is actually valid JSON by looking at the tail to see if it ends with `]`, which would indicate either a properly inserted record or some other corruption. This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   I am operating as an independent scientific reviewer (独立审查员) for the "cross_representation_disagreement_diagno" (CRDD) ML research project. My role is to independently verify experiment conclusions, check data consistency, identify bugs, and report findings exclusively via `reviewer_post_to_director`. The session involves reviewing a series of worker completions for the MVP pipeline (mvp_pipeline_004) running dual-formalization (FOL/Z3 + Python code) disagreement diagnosis on FOLIO validation set (204 samples) using Qwen3-32B-AWQ. All output must be in Chinese. I do not execute experiments or modify files — read-only access only.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Cross-representation disagreement diagnosis**: Using FOL (Z3 solver) and Python code dual formalization paths to detect and localize LLM reasoning errors
   - **MVP Gate Criteria (C1-C5)**: C1 disagree_rate≥10%, C2 diagnostic_rate≥40%, C3 error_types≥2, C4 targeted_vs_blind>5pp, C5 localization≥60%
   - **Stability check (ground-truth-free localization)**: Re-generates both representations, checks consistency to predict which side has the error — only meaningful C5 metric
   - **C5 circular reasoning bug**: `disagreement.error_side` is derived from ground truth (same computation as the "actual" value), making the comparison tautological (always 100%)
   - **JSONL streaming write vs JSON array**: Memory optimization to avoid accumulating all results in memory during resume
   - **9:1 asymmetry**: FOL correct in ~78% of disagree cases vs Code ~8.7% — stable pattern challenging "bidirectional diagnostic" framing
   - **C4 excl-Unknown FAIL**: Targeted repair advantage concentrated on Unknown-GT samples (+11.6pp overall vs +3.6pp excl-Unknown)
   - **vLLM serving**: Qwen3-32B-AWQ with awq_marlin quantization, localhost:8000, PID 19902 on GPU 0
   - **Pipeline crash pattern**: Silent exits during stability check / targeted repair phases (extra LLM calls), likely memory-related

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/autodl-tmp/crdd/src/pipeline.py` (264 lines, on autodl-rtx6000pro)
     - Main pipeline: data loading → dual formalization → execution → disagreement detection → error classification → repair → save
     - Memory leak fix added: `import gc`, `_load_done_ids()` (loads only ID set), `_convert_json_to_jsonl()`, JSONL per-line append, `gc.collect()` after each sample
     - CRITICAL BUG identified: When `--output == --resume` and file is JSON array, conversion is skipped (`if not Path(output_file).exists()` is False), JSONL append after `]` would corrupt file
     - Key code for memory-optimized resume:
       ```python
       def _load_done_ids(path: str) -> set:
           """Extract completed sample IDs from result file (JSON array or JSONL).
           Only IDs are kept in memory — full records are discarded."""
           p = Path(path)
           if not p.exists(): return set()
           done = set()
           with open(p) as f:
               first_char = f.read(1)
               if not first_char: return done
               f.seek(0)
               if first_char == '[':
                   for rec in json.load(f):
                       if "id" in rec: done.add(rec["id"])
               else:
                   for line in f:
                       line = line.strip()
                       if line:
                           rec = json.loads(line)
                           if "id" in rec: done.add(rec["id"])
           return done
       ```
     - Conversion logic with the bug:
       ```python
       if not Path(output_file).exists():  # BUG: skips when output==resume
           with open(resume_from) as f:
               first_char = f.read(1)
           if first_char == '[':
               _convert_json_to_jsonl(resume_from, output_file)
       ```
     - Write logic: `with open(output_file, "a") as f: f.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + '\n')`

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/crdd/src/evaluate_mvp.py` (on autodl-rtx6000pro)
     - MVP Gate evaluation script, computes C1-C5 with dual mode (all samples / excl GT=Unknown)
     - C5 FIX: Now uses only `stability.predicted_error_side` (ground-truth-free), skips "ambiguous"
     - Updated `load_results()` to support both JSON array and JSONL (auto-detection via first char)
     - Fixed C5 code:
       ```python
       # C5: localization accuracy (ground-truth-free prediction via stability check)
       for r in valid:
           stab = r.get("stability")
           if not isinstance(stab, dict): continue
           predicted = stab.get("predicted_error_side")
           if not predicted or predicted == "ambiguous": continue
           # ... compare predicted vs actual from fol_correct/code_correct

   - `/root/autodl-tmp/crdd/src/disagree.py` (on autodl-rtx6000pro)

{

    "content": {
      "type": "text",
      "file": {
        "filePath": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_3/projects/cross_representation_disagreement_diagno/docs/proofwriter_survey.md",
        "content": "# ProofWriter 数据集调研报告\n\n## 1. 数据集基本信息\n\n**论文**: Tafjord, Dalvi, Clark. \"ProofWriter: Generating Implications, Proofs, and Abductive Statements over Natural Language.\" Findings of ACL-IJCNLP 2021.\n\n**任务格式**: NLI 风格，与 FOLIO 一致\n- **输入**: theory（自然语言事实 + 规则）+ question（待验证陈述）\n- **输出**: True / False / Unknown（OWA 设定下）\n- **附加**: 每个样本包含推理证明（DAG 格式）\n\n**数据规模**（OWA，与我们相关的设定）:\n\n| Depth | Theories | Questions |\n|-------|----------|-----------|\n| D0 | 26,978 | 100,000 |\n| D1 | 12,933 | 100,014 |\n| D2 | 9,033 | 100,010 |\n| D3 | 6,940 | 100,036 |\n| D5 | 4,752 | 100,030 |\n\n总计 ~500k questions (OWA)。Split: 70% train / 10% dev / 20% test。无 D4。\n\n**标签分布**:\n- **OWA**: True / False / Unknown 各约 **33%**（均衡分布）\n- **CWA**: 仅 True / False（无 Unknown），与我们不兼容\n- 对比 FOLIO：~39% Unknown，ProofWriter 更均衡\n\n**4 种配置**: AttNeg（属性+否定）、AttNoneg（属性无否定）、RelNeg（关系+否定）、RelNoneg（关系无否定）\n\n## 2. 与 FOLIO 的对比\n\n| 维度 | ProofWriter | FOLIO |\n|------|------------|-------|\n| 生成方式 | **合成/模板化**（Datalog 程序生成） | **专家人工标注** |\n| 词汇量 | ~101 词 | 4,351 词 |\n| 自然语言风格 | 简单模板句：\"Charlie is quiet.\" \"If someone is kind then they are young.\" | 真实自然语言，语义复杂 |\n| 规模 | ~500k (OWA) | 1,435 样本 |\n| 标签 | True/False/Unknown (OWA) | True/False/Unknown |\n| FOL 标注 | 无 | 有 |\n| 推理深度 | 显式标注 D0-D5 | 隐式（范围 0-14） |\n| 证明 | 包含（DAG） | 无 |\n\n**核心差异**: ProofWriter 是合成数据，语言高度模板化，推理结构干净可控；FOLIO 是真实自然语言，语义歧义多，形式化难度高。两者互补性强。\n\n**FOL 形式化适配性**: ProofWriter 的规则结构极其规整（一阶 Horn 子句 + 否定），转 Z3 非常直接。事实是简单的一元/二元谓词，规则是标准的 if-then 形式。比 FOLIO 容易得多。\n\n**Python 代码形式化适配性**: 同样容易。事实可表示为集合/字典，规则可表示为函数或推理循环。模板化结构意味着 LLM 生成代码的变异性更低。\n\n## 3. 对我们方法的预期影响\n\n### 3.1 不对称性预测\n\n**核心问题**: ProofWriter 会放大还是缩小 FOLIO 上观察到的 9:1 FOL 偏向不对称性？\n\n**预测: disagree rate 会显著降低，不对称性模式可能改变。**\n\n理由：\n- ProofWriter 的模板化语言大幅降低了 NL→FOL 和 NL→Code 的翻译难度\n- FOL 和 Code 都会变得更准确 → agree rate 上升 → disagree 样本减少\n- FOLIO 的 9:1 不对称性很可能源于 FOLIO 的语义复杂性对 Code 形式化的挑战更大（自然语言歧义 → Code 更容易出错），而 ProofWriter 消除了这一因素\n\n**两种可能的结果**:\n1. **不对称性缩小**（如 6:4 或 5:5）：说明不对称性与数据集语言复杂度相关，非方法固有偏差 → 对论文有利\n2. **不对称性保持但方向可能翻转**：如果 Code 在结构化数据上反而更强 → 更有趣的发现\n\n### 3.2 推理深度作为分析轴\n\n**高价值**。可以回答：\n- 浅层推理（D0-D1）是否 agree rate 极高（两种表征都轻松搞定）？\n- 深层推理（D3-D5）是否 disagree rate 上升？哪种表征在深层推理更脆弱？\n- 这为论文提供了 \"推理复杂度 vs 表征分歧\" 的分析维度，FOLIO 无法提供\n\n### 3.3 风险\n\n- **disagree 样本太少**: 如果两种表征在简单模板数据上都接近完美，disagree 数量可能不足以支撑统计分析\n- **缓解**: 使用 D5（最深推理）+ RelNeg（关系+否定，最复杂配置）来最大化难度\n\n## 4. 实操信息\n\n### 4.1 HuggingFace 数据集\n\n**推荐: `smoorsmith/proofwriter`**\n- 4,200 行（train 3,000 / dev 600 / test 600）\n- OWA D5 子集，来自 Logic-LLM 仓库（与 FOLIO 评测对齐）\n- 字段: `id`, `context`, `question`, `options`, `answer`, `depth`, `chain_of_thought`\n- Logic-LLM 论文正是用 FOLIO + ProofWriter OWA-D5 做评测，有直接可比性\n\n**其他版本**:\n- `tasksource/proofwriter`: 845k 行，全量数据，覆盖所有深度和配置\n- `D3xter1922/proofwriter-dataset`: 59k 行，原始格式\n\n### 4.2 Pipeline 适配\n\n当前 FOLIO 格式: `{premises, conclusion, label}`\n\nProofWriter (`smoorsmith/proofwriter`) 适配：\n```\ncontext → premises（事实+规则文本）\nquestion → 需提取结论（去掉 \"Based on the above information, is the following statement true, false, or unknown?\" 前缀）\nanswer → label（True/False/Unknown）\ndepth → 额外元数据（用于分层分析）\n```\n\n改动量小，新增一个 `ProofWriterDataLoader` 即可。\n\n### 4.3 推荐评测配置\n\n| 配置 | 样本数 | 理由 |\n|------|--------|------|\n| **OWA D5 test set** | 600 | Logic-LLM 标准配置，三类标签均衡，推理深度最大 |\n| 如需缩减 | 随机采样 200-300 | 保持标签均衡采样 |\n\n**推荐方案**: 直接用 `smoorsmith/proofwriter` 的 600 test 样本，与 FOLIO 的 204 样本规模接近。\n\n## 5. 替代数据集\n\n| 数据集 | 标签 | 格式匹配度 | 优势 | 劣势 | 推荐度 |\n|--------|------|-----------|------|------|--------|\n| **ProofWriter OWA** | T/F/U | ★★★★★ | 标签一致、Logic-LLM 先例、深度轴 | 合成数据、语言简单 | **首选** |\n| LogicNLI | E/C/N→T/F/U | ★★★★ | 三类标签、合成、FOL 基础 | 知名度低、无深度标注 | 备选 |\n| RuleTaker | T/F | ★★★ | ProofWriter 前身、大规模 | 无 Unknown 类、仅 CWA | 不推荐 |\n| LogiQA 2.0 NLI | 2-way | ★★ | 真实考试题 | 非规则推理、标签不匹配 | 不推荐 |\n| CLUTRR | 关系推理 | ★ | 亲属关系推理 | 任务格式完全不同 | 不适用 |\n| PrOntoQA | T/F | ★★ | 本体层级推理 | 无 Unknown、二分类 | 不推荐 |\n\n## 6. 结论\n\n**ProofWriter OWA D5 是最佳第二数据集**。理由：\n1. 任务格式与 FOLIO 完全一致（premises → conclusion → T/F/U）\n2. Logic-LLM 已建立 FOLIO + ProofWriter 的评测先例，审稿人容易接受\n3. 合成 vs 人工标注的对比恰好验证方法在不同数据特性下的泛化性\n4. 推理深度维度提供 FOLIO 无法提供的分析轴\n5. 标签均衡（各33%）vs FOLIO 的不均衡（39% Unknown）可验证标签分布对不对称性的影响\n6. HuggingFace 上有现成数据集（`smoorsmith/proofwriter`），适配工作量极小（~1天）\n",
        "numLines": 136,
        "startLine": 1,
        "totalLines": 136
      }
    },
    "displayPath": "../../../docs/proofwriter_survey.md"
  },
